TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度异常检测框架的锂离子电池在线故障诊断方法，融合核单类分类与最小体积估计，并引入机械约束和STDP动态表征，在20辆电动汽车的860万条数据上验证，平均TPR提升7.59%，PPV提升27.92%，F1提升18.28%，AUC提升23.68%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动汽车锂离子电池在复杂现实工况下的安全故障诊断面临挑战，现有方法难以有效表征复杂故障模式并构建紧凑的正常状态边界。

Method: 提出基于核单类分类与最小体积估计结合的深度异常检测框架；引入机械约束提升故障表征能力；采用STDP动态表征构建更紧凑的正常状态边界；在潜在空间学习流形结构以增强框架鲁棒性。

Result: 在860万数据点验证，相比多个先进基线方法，平均TPR提升7.59%，PPV提升27.92%，F1提升18.28%，AUC提升23.68%；分析了故障表征的空间分离特性；揭示了不同故障类型间可能存在共享的因果结构。

Conclusion: 深度学习与物理约束及神经动力学结合为电池安全诊断提供了有效路径，该方法具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Online safety fault diagnosis is essential for lithium-ion batteries in electric vehicles(EVs), particularly under complex and rare safety-critical conditions in real-world operation. In this work, we develop an online battery fault diagnosis network based on a deep anomaly detection framework combining kernel one-class classification and minimum-volume estimation. Mechanical constraints and spike-timing-dependent plasticity(STDP)-based dynamic representations are introduced to improve complex fault characterization and enable a more compact normal-state boundary. The proposed method is validated using 8.6 million valid data points collected from 20 EVs. Compared with several advanced baseline methods, it achieves average improvements of 7.59% in TPR, 27.92% in PPV, 18.28% in F1 score, and 23.68% in AUC. In addition, we analyze the spatial separation of fault representations before and after modeling, and further enhance framework robustness by learning the manifold structure in the latent space. The results also suggest the possible presence of shared causal structures across different fault types, highlighting the promise of integrating deep learning with physical constraints and neural dynamics for battery safety diagnosis.

</details>


### [112] [SafeSeek: Universal Attribution of Safety Circuits in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23268)
*Miao Yu,Siyuan Fu,Moayad Aloqaily,Zhenhong Zhou,Safa Otoum,Xing fan,Kun Wang,Yufei Guo,Qingsong Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的安全可解释性框架，通过可微分二元掩码识别LLM中的功能完整安全电路，并通过安全电路微调实现高效安全对齐。在后门攻击和安全性对齐两个场景中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全归因方法依赖启发式、领域特定的指标和搜索算法，泛化能力和可靠性不足。

Method: 引入可微分二元掩码（differentiable binary masks），通过在安全数据集上梯度下降提取多粒度电路，并结合安全电路微调（Safety Circuit Tuning）进行高效安全微调。

Result: 后门攻击场景：识别0.42%稀疏度的后门电路，消融后攻击成功率从100%降至0.4%，同时保持99%以上通用能力。安全对齐场景：定位3.03%注意力头和0.79%神经元的对齐电路，移除后ASR从0.8%飙升至96.9%，而在有帮助性微调中排除该电路仍保持96.5%安全保留率。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效识别LLM中功能完整的安全电路，为理解和干预模型安全行为提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability reveals that safety-critical behaviors (e.g., alignment, jailbreak, backdoor) in Large Language Models (LLMs) are grounded in specialized functional components. However, existing safety attribution methods struggle with generalization and reliability due to their reliance on heuristic, domain-specific metrics and search algorithms. To address this, we propose \ourmethod, a unified safety interpretability framework that identifies functionally complete safety circuits in LLMs via optimization. Unlike methods focusing on isolated heads or neurons, \ourmethod introduces differentiable binary masks to extract multi-granular circuits through gradient descent on safety datasets, while integrates Safety Circuit Tuning to utilize these sparse circuits for efficient safety fine-tuning. We validate \ourmethod in two key scenarios in LLM safety: \textbf{(1) backdoor attacks}, identifying a backdoor circuit with 0.42\% sparsity, whose ablation eradicates the Attack Success Rate (ASR) from 100\% $\to$ 0.4\% while retaining over 99\% general utility; \textbf{(2) safety alignment}, localizing an alignment circuit with 3.03\% heads and 0.79\% neurons, whose removal spikes ASR from 0.8\% $\to$ 96.9\%, whereas excluding this circuit during helpfulness fine-tuning maintains 96.5\% safety retention.

</details>


### [113] [A Comparative Study of Machine Learning Models for Hourly Forecasting of Air Temperature and Relative Humidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23282)
*Jiaqi Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究对比了7种机器学习模型在重庆（山地城市）进行空气温度和相对湿度短时预测的性能。XGBoost表现最佳，测试MAE分别为0.302°C和1.271%，平均R²达0.989，证明了树集成方法对山地城市气象时序预测的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 山地城市（如重庆）地形复杂，气象要素时空变异性大，准确的短期温湿度预测对城市管理至关重要。现有研究缺乏对多种机器学习方法在复杂地形条件下的系统性对比评估。

Method: 研究采用统一框架，包括：数据预处理、滞后特征构建、滚动统计特征提取及时序交叉验证。对比了7种模型：XGBoost、随机森林、SVR、MLP、决策树、LSTM及CNN-LSTM，从预测精度和鲁棒性两方面进行系统评估。

Result: XGBoost取得最佳综合表现。空气温度预测测试MAE为0.302°C，相对湿度预测MAE为1.271%，两项任务平均R²达0.989。树集成方法在结构化气象时序预测任务中优于深度学习方法。

Conclusion: XGBoost等树集成学习方法在山地城市气象时序预测中具有显著优势，可为山地城市智能气象预报系统建设提供技术支撑和实践指导。

Abstract: Accurate short-term forecasting of air temperature and relative humidity is critical for urban management, especially in topographically complex cities such as Chongqing, China. This study compares seven machine learning models: eXtreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost), Random Forest, Support Vector Regression (SVR), Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP), Decision Tree, Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks, and Convolutional Neural Network (CNN)-LSTM (CNN-LSTM), for hourly prediction using real-world open data. Based on a unified framework of data preprocessing, lag-feature construction, rolling statistical features, and time-series validation, the models are systematically evaluated in terms of predictive accuracy and robustness. The results show that XGBoost achieves the best overall performance, with a test mean absolute error (MAE) of 0.302 °C for air temperature and 1.271% for relative humidity, together with an average R2 of 0.989 across the two forecasting tasks. These findings demonstrate the strong effectiveness of tree-based ensemble learning for structured meteorological time-series forecasting and provide practical guidance for intelligent meteorological forecasting in mountainous cities.

</details>


### [114] [Robustness Quantification for Discriminative Models: a New Robustness Metric and its Application to Dynamic Classifier Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23318)
*Rodrigo F. L. Lassance,Jasper De Bock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的鲁棒性度量方法，适用于任何概率判别分类器和任何类型特征，可用于区分可靠与不可靠预测，并指导动态分类器选择策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒性量化方法需要使用生成模型，且仅限于特定模型架构或离散特征，限制了其在实际应用中的广泛适用性。

Method: 提出一种新型鲁棒性度量指标，通过评估分类器在改变预测前能承受的不确定性程度来量化预测可靠性。

Result: 该度量能够有效区分可靠预测与不可靠预测。

Conclusion: 该方法可推广至任意概率判别分类器和任意类型特征，并可用于开发动态分类器选择策略。

Abstract: Among the different possible strategies for evaluating the reliability of individual predictions of classifiers, robustness quantification stands out as a method that evaluates how much uncertainty a classifier could cope with before changing its prediction. However, its applicability is more limited than some of its alternatives, since it requires the use of generative models and restricts the analyses either to specific model architectures or discrete features. In this work, we propose a new robustness metric applicable to any probabilistic discriminative classifier and any type of features. We demonstrate that this new metric is capable of distinguishing between reliable and unreliable predictions, and use this observation to develop new strategies for dynamic classifier selection.

</details>


### [115] [Off-Policy Value-Based Reinforcement Learning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23355)
*Peng-Yuan Wang,Ziniu Li,Tian Xu,Bohan Yang,Tian-Shuo Liu,ChenYang Wang,Xiong-Hui Chen,Yi-Chen Li,Tianyun Yang,Congliang Chen,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReVal是一种基于Bellman更新的价值型RL框架，通过结合步进信号和轨迹级验证信号，支持离线学习，能高效复用历史轨迹。在数学推理任务上，ReVal在收敛速度和最终性能上均优于GRPO方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM的RL训练方法（如GRPO）主要是on-policy的，每批数据只用一次就丢弃，样本效率低下。对于长时域任务，轨迹生成成本高，现有方法数据利用率严重不足。

Method: 提出ReVal方法：1) 基于Bellman更新的价值型RL框架；2) 结合步进信号（捕获内部一致性）和轨迹级信号（来自结果验证）；3) 支持基于回放缓冲区的训练，实现历史轨迹的高效复用。

Result: 在数学推理基准测试中，ReVal不仅收敛更快，最终性能也优于GRPO。在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-1.5B上，AIME24提升2.7%，域外基准GPQA提升4.5%。

Conclusion: 价值型RL是LLM训练中策略型方法的有效替代方案，具有更高的训练效率和更好的性能表现。

Abstract: Improving data utilization efficiency is critical for scaling reinforcement learning (RL) for long-horizon tasks where generating trajectories is expensive. However, the dominant RL methods for LLMs are largely on-policy: they update each batch of data only once, discard it, and then collect fresh samples, resulting in poor sample efficiency. In this work, we explore an alternative value-based RL framework for LLMs that naturally enables off-policy learning. We propose ReVal, a Bellman-update-based method that combines stepwise signals capturing internal consistency with trajectory-level signals derived from outcome verification. ReVal naturally supports replay-buffer-based training, allowing efficient reuse of past trajectories. Experiments on standard mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that ReVal not only converges faster but also outperforms GRPO in final performance. On DeepSeek-R1-Distill-1.5B, ReVal improves training efficiency and achieves improvement of 2.7% in AIME24 and 4.5% in out-of-domain benchmark GPQA over GRPO. These results suggest that value-based RL is a practical alternative to policy-based methods for LLM training.

</details>


### [116] [Central Dogma Transformer III: Interpretable AI Across DNA, RNA, and Protein](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23361)
*Nobuyuki Ota*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CDT-III是一个跨中心法则(DNA-RNA-蛋白质)的机制导向AI模型，采用双阶段虚拟细胞嵌入器架构(VCE-N核内转录、VCE-C胞质翻译)。在5个保留基因上达到RNA r=0.843、蛋白 r=0.969的预测精度，证明下游任务可正则化上游表征，并实现仅用基线数据即可筛选2361个基因的副作用预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物AI模型的表征与分子过程脱节，缺乏机制可解释性；传统方法难以在无实验数据条件下进行大规模基因功能筛选。

Method: 提出CDT-III，采用双阶段Virtual Cell Embedder架构：VCE-N建模核内转录过程，VCE-C建模胞质翻译过程，模拟细胞空间区室化特性。通过蛋白质监督增强DNA层面的可解释性，利用梯度分析方法进行副作用剖析。

Result: (1) 5个保留基因上RNA r=0.843、蛋白 r=0.969；(2) 蛋白预测任务可提升RNA性能(0.804→0.843)，证明跨层次正则化；(3) 蛋白监督使CTCF富集度提升30%；(4) CD52基因knockdown模拟中正确预测29/29蛋白变化，重发现5/7临床副作用；(5) 仅用基线数据副作用预测r=0.939，可筛选全部2361个基因。

Conclusion: CDT-III成功桥接中心法则各层次表征学习，实现了机制可解释的虚拟细胞建模；下游任务的监督信号可有效正则化上游表征；梯度分析结合基线数据即可实现大规模基因功能筛选，具有临床药物副作用预测的应用潜力。

Abstract: Biological AI models increasingly predict complex cellular responses, yet their learned representations remain disconnected from the molecular processes they aim to capture. We present CDT-III, which extends mechanism-oriented AI across the full central dogma: DNA, RNA, and protein. Its two-stage Virtual Cell Embedder architecture mirrors the spatial compartmentalization of the cell: VCE-N models transcription in the nucleus and VCE-C models translation in the cytosol. On five held-out genes, CDT-III achieves per-gene RNA r=0.843 and protein r=0.969. Adding protein prediction improves RNA performance (r=0.804 to 0.843), demonstrating that downstream tasks regularize upstream representations. Protein supervision sharpens DNA-level interpretability, increasing CTCF enrichment by 30%. Applied to in silico CD52 knockdown approximating Alemtuzumab, the model predicts 29/29 protein changes correctly and rediscovers 5 of 7 known clinical side effects without clinical data. Gradient-based side effect profiling requires only unperturbed baseline data (r=0.939), enabling screening of all 2,361 genes without new experiments.

</details>


### [117] [Graph Energy Matching: Transport-Aligned Energy-Based Modeling for Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23398)
*Michal Balcerak,Suprosana Shit,Chinmay Prabhakar,Sebastian Kaltenbach,Michael S. Albergo,Yilun Du,Bjoern Menze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Graph Energy Matching (GEM)框架，解决离散能量模型在图生成中因虚假局部极小值导致采样困难和训练不稳定的问题。GEM基于JKO输运映射优化，通过学习置换不变势能同时引导噪声向数据迁移并细化高似然区域样本，并引入能量切换采样协议平衡快速输运与探索混合。在分子图基准上达到或超越离散扩散模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散能量模型虽然能显式捕获相对似然、支持可组合的概率推理任务（如条件生成、约束强化），但其采样效率和质量受制于off-support区域的虚假局部极小值，导致采样器被困住和训练不稳定，相比离散扩散模型存在显著保真度差距。

Method: 提出Graph Energy Matching (GEM)框架，核心包括：(1) 基于Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto (JKO)输运映射优化视角，学习置换不变势能函数，同时提供从噪声到数据的输运对齐引导，并在高数据似然区域内细化样本；(2) 引入基于能量切换的采样协议，无缝桥接两个阶段——快速梯度引导的输运阶段（趋近高概率区域）和混合 regime（探索学习到的图分布）。

Result: 在分子图基准数据集上，GEM达到或超越强离散扩散基线模型。额外展示了显式相对似然建模带来的能力：目标导向探索、属性约束采样、图之间的测地线插值。

Conclusion: GEM成功弥合了离散能量模型与离散扩散模型之间的保真度差距，保留了能量模型的可组合概率推理优势，同时实现了高质量采样。

Abstract: Energy-based models for discrete domains, such as graphs, explicitly capture relative likelihoods, naturally enabling composable probabilistic inference tasks like conditional generation or enforcing constraints at test-time. However, discrete energy-based models typically struggle with efficient and high-quality sampling, as off-support regions often contain spurious local minima, trapping samplers and causing training instabilities. This has historically resulted in a fidelity gap relative to discrete diffusion models. We introduce Graph Energy Matching (GEM), a generative framework for graphs that closes this fidelity gap. Motivated by the transport map optimization perspective of the Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto (JKO) scheme, GEM learns a permutation-invariant potential energy that simultaneously provides transport-aligned guidance from noise toward data and refines samples within regions of high data likelihood. Further, we introduce a sampling protocol that leverages an energy-based switch to seamlessly bridge: (i) rapid, gradient-guided transport toward high-probability regions to (ii) a mixing regime for exploration of the learned graph distribution. On molecular graph benchmarks, GEM matches or exceeds strong discrete diffusion baselines. Beyond sample quality, explicit modeling of relative likelihood enables targeted exploration at inference time, facilitating compositional generation, property-constrained sampling, and geodesic interpolation between graphs.

</details>


### [118] [SortedRL: Accelerating RL Training for LLMs through Online Length-Aware Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23414)
*Yiqi Zhang,Huiqiang Jiang,Xufang Luo,Zhihe Yang,Chengruidong Zhang,Yifei Shen,Dongsheng Li,Yuqing Yang,Lili Qiu,Yang You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SortedRL通过长度感知的在线调度策略解决RL训练中rollout阶段的效率瓶颈问题，通过重排序短序列样本实现更快的策略更新，同时保持训练稳定性，在长思维链任务中可减少50%以上的训练气泡比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在提升大语言模型推理能力方面展现出巨大潜力，但RL训练效率受到rollout阶段的严重制约。当生成较长轨迹（如16k tokens）时，rollout阶段可占据总训练时间的70%，主要原因是自回归生成的固有延迟以及rollout与策略更新之间的同步开销。

Method: 提出SortedRL，一种在线长度感知的调度策略。核心方法包括：(1) 根据输出长度重排序rollout样本，优先处理短样本形成批次以加速更新；(2) 支持大规模rollout批次和灵活的更新批次配置；(3) 引入基于缓存的机制控制off-policy训练程度；(4) 配套专用RL基础设施，通过有状态控制器和rollout缓冲区管理训练流程。

Result: 使用LLaMA-3.1-8B和Qwen-2.5-32B在逻辑谜题、AIME 24、Math 500和Minerval等数学推理任务上进行实验验证。结果表明：训练气泡比降低超过50%，在相同数据量下相比基线模型获得3.9%至18.4%的性能提升。

Conclusion: SortedRL通过长度感知调度策略有效缓解了长思维链RL训练中的rollout效率瓶颈，兼顾了训练效率和稳定性，为大规模LLM推理能力提升提供了一种高效的RL训练范式。

Abstract: Scaling reinforcement learning (RL) has shown strong promise for enhancing the reasoning abilities of large language models (LLMs), particularly in tasks requiring long chain-of-thought generation. However, RL training efficiency is often bottlenecked by the rollout phase, which can account for up to 70% of total training time when generating long trajectories (e.g., 16k tokens), due to slow autoregressive generation and synchronization overhead between rollout and policy updates. We propose SortedRL, an online length-aware scheduling strategy designed to address this bottleneck by improving rollout efficiency and maintaining training stability. SortedRL reorders rollout samples based on output lengths, prioritizing short samples forming groups for early updates. This enables large rollout batches, flexible update batches, and near on-policy micro-curriculum construction simultaneously. To further accelerate the pipeline, SortedRL incorporates a mechanism to control the degree of off-policy training through a cache-based mechanism, and is supported by a dedicated RL infrastructure that manages rollout and update via a stateful controller and rollout buffer. Experiments using LLaMA-3.1-8B and Qwen-2.5-32B on diverse tasks, including logical puzzles, and math challenges like AIME 24, Math 500, and Minerval, show that SortedRL reduces RL training bubble ratios by over 50%, while attaining 3.9% to 18.4% superior performance over baseline given same amount of data.

</details>


### [119] [Similarity-Aware Mixture-of-Experts for Data-Efficient Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23436)
*Connor Mclaughlin,Nigel Lee,Lili Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决持续学习中数据有限且任务任意重叠的难题，提出基于预训练模型的自适应MoE框架，通过增量全局池化和实例级提示掩码实现有效知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法存在假设过强的问题——假设任务数据充足且任务不重叠，无法应对现实世界中数据稀缺且任务重叠的挑战。

Method: 提出自适应混合专家框架，包含增量全局池化（缓解提示关联噪声）和实例级提示掩码（区分分布内外样本）两个核心组件。

Result: 实验表明方法在多种数据量和任务相似性条件下有效提升样本效率。

Conclusion: 该框架能够充分利用潜在的任务重叠，同时在数据稀缺情况下主动防止负迁移。

Abstract: Machine learning models often need to adapt to new data after deployment due to structured or unstructured real-world dynamics. The Continual Learning (CL) framework enables continuous model adaptation, but most existing approaches either assume each task contains sufficiently many data samples or that the learning tasks are non-overlapping. In this paper, we address the more general setting where each task may have a limited dataset, and tasks may overlap in an arbitrary manner without a priori knowledge. This general setting is substantially more challenging for two reasons. On the one hand, data scarcity necessitates effective contextualization of general knowledge and efficient knowledge transfer across tasks. On the other hand, unstructured task overlapping can easily result in negative knowledge transfer. To address the above challenges, we propose an adaptive mixture-of-experts (MoE) framework over pre-trained models that progressively establishes similarity awareness among tasks. Our design contains two innovative algorithmic components: incremental global pooling and instance-wise prompt masking. The former mitigates prompt association noise through gradual prompt introduction over time. The latter decomposes incoming task samples into those aligning with current prompts (in-distribution) and those requiring new prompts (out-of-distribution). Together, our design strategically leverages potential task overlaps while actively preventing negative mutual interference in the presence of per-task data scarcity. Experiments across varying data volumes and inter-task similarity show that our method enhances sample efficiency and is broadly applicable.

</details>


### [120] [End-to-End Efficient RL for Linear Bellman Complete MDPs with Deterministic Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23461)
*Zakaria Mhammedi,Alexander Rakhlin,Nneka Okolo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究满足线性Bellman完备性的马尔可夫决策过程中的线性函数近似强化学习问题，提出了一个计算高效的算法，适用于确定性转移、随机初始状态和随机奖励的环境，在有限动作空间端到端高效，在大/无限动作空间仅需标准argmax oracle，样本和计算复杂度关于水平线、特征维度和1/ε多项式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在满足线性Bellman完备性的马尔可夫决策过程中的应用虽然统计上可处理，但现有计算高效算法存在局限：要么局限于小动作空间，要么需要对特征空间有强oracle假设。

Method: 提出了一个计算高效算法，适用于线性Bellman完备MDP，具有确定性转移、随机初始状态和随机奖励的特点。算法在有限动作空间端到端高效，在大/无限动作空间仅需标准argmax oracle。

Result: 算法能够学习到ε-最优策略，样本复杂度和计算复杂度均为关于水平线、特征维度和1/ε的多项式。

Conclusion: 本文算法在计算效率和适用范围上均有提升，填补了线性Bellman完备MDP中大规模动作空间场景下高效算法的空白。

Abstract: We study reinforcement learning (RL) with linear function approximation in Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) satisfying \emph{linear Bellman completeness} -- a fundamental setting where the Bellman backup of any linear value function remains linear. While statistically tractable, prior computationally efficient algorithms are either limited to small action spaces or require strong oracle assumptions over the feature space. We provide a computationally efficient algorithm for linear Bellman complete MDPs with \emph{deterministic transitions}, stochastic initial states, and stochastic rewards. For finite action spaces, our algorithm is end-to-end efficient; for large or infinite action spaces, we require only a standard argmax oracle over actions. Our algorithm learns an $\varepsilon$-optimal policy with sample and computational complexity polynomial in the horizon, feature dimension, and $1/\varepsilon$.

</details>


### [121] [Byzantine-Robust and Differentially Private Federated Optimization under Weaker Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23472)
*Rustem Islamov,Grigory Malinovsky,Alexander Gaponov,Aurelien Lucchi,Peter Richtárik,Eduard Gorbunov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Byz-Clip21-SGD2M算法，将鲁棒聚合、双动量和精心设计的梯度裁剪相结合，在标准L-平滑和σ-次高斯梯度噪声假设下提供高概率收敛保证，同时改善了拜占庭攻击和差分隐私设置下的效用，并在MNIST数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽能避免原始数据集中，但梯度更新仍可能泄露敏感信息，且恶意服务器可发动拜占庭攻击。现有的拜占庭鲁棒与差分隐私统一框架存在诸多局限：依赖有界梯度等不切实际的假设、需要辅助服务器端数据集、或缺乏收敛保证。

Method: 提出Byz-Clip21-SGD2M算法，核心创新包括：(1) 鲁棒聚合机制抵抗拜占庭攻击；(2) 双动量(Dual Momentum)加速收敛；(3) 精心设计的梯度裁剪实现差分隐私保护。理论分析基于L-平滑性和σ-次高斯梯度噪声标准假设。

Result: 在标准假设下证明了高概率收敛保证；无对抗设置下恢复SOTA收敛速率；在拜占庭和差分隐私设置下改善了效用保证。

Conclusion: Byz-Clip21-SGD2M成功在统一框架中整合了拜占庭鲁棒性与差分隐私，理论验证其收敛性，MNIST数据集上CNN和MLP模型的实验结果进一步证实了其有效性。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables heterogeneous clients to collaboratively train a shared model without centralizing their raw data, offering an inherent level of privacy. However, gradients and model updates can still leak sensitive information, while malicious servers may mount adversarial attacks such as Byzantine manipulation. These vulnerabilities highlight the need to address differential privacy (DP) and Byzantine robustness within a unified framework. Existing approaches, however, often rely on unrealistic assumptions such as bounded gradients, require auxiliary server-side datasets, or fail to provide convergence guarantees. We address these limitations by proposing Byz-Clip21-SGD2M, a new algorithm that integrates robust aggregation with double momentum and carefully designed clipping. We prove high-probability convergence guarantees under standard $L$-smoothness and $σ$-sub-Gaussian gradient noise assumptions, thereby relaxing conditions that dominate prior work. Our analysis recovers state-of-the-art convergence rates in the absence of adversaries and improves utility guarantees under Byzantine and DP settings. Empirical evaluations on CNN and MLP models trained on MNIST further validate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [122] [Estimating Flow Velocity and Vehicle Angle-of-Attack from Non-invasive Piezoelectric Structural Measurements Using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23496)
*Chandler B. Smith,S. Hales Swift,Andrew Steyer,Ihab El-Kady*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Accurate estimation of aerodynamic state variables such as freestream velocity and angle of attack (AoA) is important for aerodynamic load prediction, flight control, and model validation. This work presents a non-intrusive method for estimating vehicle velocity and AoA from structural vibration measurements rather than direct flow instrumentation such as pitot tubes. A dense array of piezoelectric sensors mounted on the interior skin of an aeroshell capture vibrations induced by turbulent boundary layer pressure fluctuations, and a convolutional neural network (CNN) is trained to invert these structural responses to recover velocity and AoA.
  Proof-of-concept is demonstrated through controlled experiments in Sandia's hypersonic wind tunnel spanning zero and nonzero AoA configurations, Mach~5 and Mach~8 conditions, and both constant and continuously varying tunnel operations. The CNN is trained and evaluated using data from 16 wind tunnel runs, with a temporally centered held-out interval within each run used to form training, validation, and test datasets and assess intra-run temporal generalization. Raw CNN predictions exhibit increased variance during continuously varying conditions; a short-window moving-median post-processing step suppresses this variance and improves robustness. After post-processing, the method achieves a mean velocity error relative to the low-pass filtered reference velocity below 2.27~m/s (0.21\%) and a mean AoA error of $0.44^{\circ} (8.25\%)$ on held-out test data from the same experimental campaign, demonstrating feasibility of vibration-based velocity and AoA estimation in a controlled laboratory environment.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Early Discoveries of Algorithmist I: Promise of Provable Algorithm Synthesis at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22363)
*Janardhan Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Algorithmist，一个基于GitHub Copilot的自主研究智能体，通过多智能体研究与评审循环，自动设计具有可证明保证的实用算法，在差分隐私数据分析和聚类任务上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合最坏情况理论与实际性能之间的差距存在困难。现有方法（如超最坏情况分析和数据驱动算法选择）通常需要先验分布知识或限制于固定算法池。LLM的最新进展为实时可证明算法综合提供了新可能。

Method: 构建Algorithmist：一个基于GitHub Copilot的自主研究智能体，运行多智能体研究与评审循环，包含创意生成、算法与证明开发、证明指导的实现、以及证明、代码及其一致性评审等独立阶段。采用"先证明后编码"的范式，代码与结构化自然语言证明中间表示同步开发并保持对齐。

Result: 在私有数据分析和聚类任务上，当要求设计满足隐私、近似和可解释性要求的实用方法时，系统产生了可证明正确且经验有效的算法、类研究论文的写作和经过审计的实现。在某些设置下找到了改进的算法，在另一些设置中解释了原则性障碍，并发现了先前已发表工作中的细微证明错误。

Conclusion: LLM系统可生成针对每个数据集和部署场景量身定制的研究论文级算法工件。证明了"先证明后编码"代码综合范式的可行性，其中代码与结构化自然语言证明中间表示并行开发并保持一致。

Abstract: Designing algorithms with provable guarantees that also work well in practice remains difficult, requiring both mathematical reasoning and careful implementation. Existing approaches that bridge worst-case theory and empirical performance, such as beyond-worst-case analysis and data-driven algorithm selection, typically assume prior distributional knowledge or restrict attention to a fixed pool of algorithms. Recent progress in LLMs suggests a new possibility: provable algorithm synthesis on the fly. To study this, we built Algorithmist, an autonomous researcher agent on top of GitHub Copilot that runs a multi-agent research-and-review loop, with separate stages for idea generation, algorithm and proof development, proof-guided implementation, and review of proofs, code, and their alignment. We evaluate Algorithmist on research-level tasks in private data analysis and clustering. When asked to design practical methods that jointly satisfy privacy, approximation, and interpretability requirements, it produced provably sound and empirically effective algorithms, together with research-style writeups and audited implementations. It also found improved algorithms in some settings, explained principled barriers in others, and uncovered a subtle proof bug in prior published work. More broadly, our results suggest a new paradigm in which LLM systems generate research-paper-quality algorithmic artifacts tailored to each dataset and deployment setting. They also point to a proof-first code-synthesis paradigm, in which code is developed alongside a structured natural-language proof intermediate representation and kept aligned with it throughout synthesis.

</details>


### [124] [SkillClone: Multi-Modal Clone Detection and Clone Propagation Analysis in the Agent Skill Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22447)
*Jiaying Zhu,Lyuye Zhang,Wenbo Guo,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SkillClone is the first multi-modal clone detection system for agent skills, combining TF-IDF similarity with per-channel (YAML, NL, code) decomposition via logistic regression to detect and classify clone relationships across the tri-modal skill structure.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent skills (multi-modal packages with YAML metadata, NL instructions, and code) have grown to 196K instances, but no clone detection exists for them. Existing code clone detectors cannot handle the multi-modal nature of skills where clone evidence is distributed across three interleaved channels, creating systemic risks where vulnerabilities in copied skills silently persist.

Method: SkillClone fuses flat TF-IDF similarity with per-channel decomposition across YAML, natural language, and code channels through logistic regression. This architecture combines strong clone detection capability with interpretable clone type classification. The approach is evaluated on SkillClone-Bench, a balanced benchmark of 300 ground-truth pairs with stratified difficulty.

Result: On SkillClone-Bench, SkillClone achieves F1 of 0.939 with precision 0.952, outperforming flat TF-IDF (F1 = 0.881) and achieving 4.2x higher Type-4 (semantic) recall than MinHash. Application to 20K skills reveals 258K clone pairs involving 75% of skills, with 40% crossing author boundaries. The ecosystem is inflated 3.5x: only 5,642 unique concepts underlie 20K skills, and 41% of skills in clone families are superseded by better variants.

Conclusion: SkillClone effectively addresses multi-modal clone detection for agent skills. The large-scale analysis reveals significant redundancy in the skill ecosystem, with most skills involved in clone relationships and a substantial portion being superseded by better variants, indicating a need for better deduplication and quality control mechanisms in agent skill repositories.

Abstract: Agent skills are modular instruction packages that combine YAML metadata, natural language instructions, and embedded code, and they have reached 196K publicly available instances, yet no mechanism exists to detect clone relationships among them. This gap creates systemic risks: a vulnerability in a widely copied skill silently persists across derivatives with no alert to maintainers. Existing clone detectors, designed for single-modality source code, cannot handle the multi-modal structure of skills, where clone evidence is distributed across three interleaved content channels. We present SkillClone, the first multi-modal clone detection approach for agent skills. SkillClone fuses flat TF-IDF similarity with per-channel decomposition (YAML, NL, code) through logistic regression, combining strong detection with interpretable type classification. We construct SkillClone-Bench, a balanced benchmark of 300 ground-truth pairs with stratified difficulty. On SkillClone-Bench, SkillClone achieves F1 of 0.939 with precision 0.952, outperforming flat TF-IDF (F1 = 0.881) and achieving 4.2x higher Type-4 (semantic) recall than MinHash. Applying SkillClone to 20K skills reveals 258K clone pairs involving 75% of all skills, with 40% crossing author boundaries. A deduplication analysis shows the ecosystem is inflated 3.5x: only 5,642 unique skill concepts underlie the 20K listed skills, and 41% of skills in clone families are superseded by a strictly better variant.

</details>


### [125] [From Brittle to Robust: Improving LLM Annotations for SE Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22474)
*Lohith Senthilkumar,Tim Menzies*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SynthCore是一种新的LLM提示策略,通过将多个独立的few-shot学习器的结果聚合为集成模型,解决了LLM标注高维多目标优化问题时的盲点。该方法简单高效(无需链式思维或多智能体辩论),在49个软件工程多目标优化任务上表现优于高斯过程模型、Tree of Parzen估计器和主动学习器等先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件分析通常依赖标注数据,但人工标注耗时、昂贵且容易出错。虽然LLM可用于辅助标注,但现有方法在处理高维多目标优化问题时效果不佳,存在明显盲点。

Method: SynthCore通过让多个LLM独立生成few-shot学习意见(相互不知晓彼此答案),将这些分离的意见组合成few-shot学习器集成。方法简单,无需交叉验证或多轮交互,仅聚合多个单次提示会话的结果。

Result: 在49个软件工程多目标优化任务(涵盖软件项目管理、Makefile配置、超参数优化等)上,SynthCore在探索和开发两种模式下均优于高斯过程模型、Tree of Parzen估计器和主动学习器等先进替代方案,且全程仅使用LLM标注数据,无需人工意见。

Conclusion: few-shot学习器集成可成功标注高维多目标任务。其他成功的few-shot提示结果也可通过SynthCore的集成方法快速简便地提升性能。

Abstract: Software analytics often builds from labeled data. Labeling can be slow, error prone, and expensive. When human expertise is scarce, SE researchers sometimes ask large language models (LLMs) for the missing labels. While this has been successful in some domains, recent results show that LLM-based labeling has blind spots. Specifically, their labeling is not effective for higher dimensional multi-objective problems. To address this task, we propose a novel LLM prompting strategy called SynthCore. When one opinion fails, SynthCore's combines multiple separated opinions generated by LLMs (with no knowledge of each others' answers) into an ensemble of few-shot learners. Simpler than other strategies (e.g. chain-of-thought, multi-agent-debate, etc) SynthCore aggregates results from multiple single prompt sessions (with no crossover between them). SynthCore has been tested on 49 SE multi-objective optimization tasks, handling tasks as diverse as software project management, Makefile configuration, and hyperparameter optimization. SynthCore's ensemble found optimizations that are better than state-of-the-art alternative approaches (Gaussian Process Models, Tree of Parzen Estimators, active learners in both exploration and exploitation mode). Importantly, these optimizations were made using data labeled by LLMs, without any human opinions. From these experiments, we conclude that ensembles of few shot learners can successfully annotate high dimensional multi-objective tasks. Further, we speculate that other successful few-shot prompting results could be quickly and easily enhanced using SynthCore's ensemble approach. To support open science, all our data and scripts are available at https://github.com/lohithsowmiyan/lazy-llm/tree/clusters.

</details>


### [126] [Investigating Technical Debt Types, Issues, and Solutions in Serverless Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22480)
*Hasini Sumalee Perera,Zadia Codabux,Fabio Palomba*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究使用Stack Overflow数据，通过深度学习方法分析Serverless架构中的技术债务（TD）。对78,867个问题进行分类，发现37%与TD相关，识别出6个Serverless特有的问题类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Serverless架构日益普及，理解其技术债务的表现形式、挑战和解决方案变得至关重要。现有研究虽已探讨Serverless中的不良实践，但缺乏对TD具体呈现方式、相关问题和解决方案的系统性理解。

Method: 从Stack Overflow收集78,867个Serverless相关问题，采用深度学习模型将其标注为TD或非TD类别，并进行深入的内容分析以识别TD类型、关联问题和现有解决方案。

Result: 研究发现37%的Serverless问题与TD相关，识别出6个Serverless特有的问题类别。

Conclusion: 研究强调了开发有效检测Serverless应用中TD工具的迫切需求，为后续工具研发和最佳实践制定奠定了基础。

Abstract: Serverless computing is a cloud execution model where developers run code, and the server management is handled by the cloud provider. Serverless computing is increasingly gaining popularity as more systems adopt it to enhance scalability and reduce operational costs. While it has numerous benefits, it also embodies unique challenges inherent to serverless computing. One such challenge is Technical Debt (TD), which is exacerbated by the complexities of the serverless paradigm. While prior work has investigated the activities and bad practices that lead to TD in serverless computing, there remains a gap in understanding how TD manifests, the challenges it poses, and the solutions proposed to address TD issues in serverless systems. This study aims to investigate TD in the serverless context using Stack Overflow (SO) as a knowledge base. We collected 78,867 serverless questions on SO and labeled them as TD or non-TD using deep learning. Moreover, we conducted an in-depth analysis to identify types of TD in serverless settings, associated issues, and proposed solutions. We found that 37% of the serverless questions on SO are TD-related. We also identified six serverless-specific issues. Our research highlights the need for tools that can effectively detect TD in serverless applications.

</details>


### [127] [A Practical Framework for Flaky Failure Triage in Distributed Database Continuous Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23054)
*Jun-Peng Zhu,Qizhi Wang,Yulong Zhai,Yishen Sun,Sen Chen,Kai Xu,Peng Cai,Hongming Zhang,Heng Long,Liu Tang,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SCOUT是一个面向分布式数据库CI的实用化因果在线不确定性校准分诊框架，使用仅因果特征在CPU上实现1.17ms P95延迟，有效解决 flaky 测试分诊难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式数据库CI中 flaky 故障分诊面临严峻挑战：需在CPU-only毫秒级预算内快速决策；现有方法依赖事后产物、在数据漂移下校准差、或受有限重试策略生成标签的偏差影响。

Method: SCOUT采用严格因果特征（故障前遥测+历史数据）实现无前瞻在线决策；结合轻量级状态感知评分与稀疏元数据融合；应用事后校准支持跨时空和跨域迁移的固定阈值决策；引入后验软校正减少有限重试预算引入的标签偏差。

Result: 在3,680个标注失败运行（含462个flaky正例）和62个遥测/上下文特征上验证；扩展至TiDB v7/v8及GitHub Actions元数据轨迹研究。生产环境P95延迟1.17ms。

Conclusion: SCOUT证明了因果特征与不确定性校准相结合在实际CI场景中的有效性和实用性，为分布式数据库持续集成提供了一种可靠、高效的flaky故障自动分诊方案。

Abstract: Flaky failure triage is crucial for keeping distributed database continuous integration (CI) efficient and reliable. After a failure is observed, operators must quickly decide whether to auto-rerun the job as likely flaky or escalate it as likely persistent, often under CPU-only millisecond budgets. Existing approaches remain difficult to deploy in this setting because they may rely on post-failure artifacts, produce poorly calibrated scores under telemetry and workload shifts, or learn from labels generated by finite rerun policies. To address these challenges, we present SCOUT, a practical state-aware causal online uncertainty-calibrated triage framework for distributed database CI. SCOUT uses only strict-causal features, including pre-failure telemetry and strictly historical data, to make online decisions without lookahead. Specifically, SCOUT combines lightweight state-aware scoring with optional sparse metadata fusion, applies post-hoc calibration to support fixed-threshold decisions across temporal and cross-domain shifts, and introduces a posterior-soft correction to reduce label bias induced by finite rerun budgets. We evaluated SCOUT on a benchmark of 3,680 labeled failed runs, including 462 flaky positives, and 62 telemetry/context features. Further, we studied the feasibility of SCOUT on TiDB v7/v8 and a large GitHub Actions metadata-only trace. The experimental results demonstrated its effectiveness and usefulness. We deployed SCOUT in the production environment, achieving an end-to-end P95 latency of 1.17 ms on CPU.

</details>


### [128] [LLMON: An LLM-native Markup Language to Leverage Structure and Semantics at the LLM Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22519)
*Michael Hind,Basel Shbita,Bo Wu,Farhan Ahmed,Chad DeLuca,Nathan Fulton,David Cox,Dan Gutfreund*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向LLM的标记语言LLMON（LLM Object Notation），用于在文本中嵌入结构化和语义元数据，从而改善模型训练、提示工程和推理实现，提升准确性、安全性和隐私性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM使用纯文本作为输入输出接口，但实际信息往往具有更丰富的结构和语义（如指令与数据的区分），这些信息在字符串形式中未被传达，导致模型混淆和安全风险（如提示注入攻击）。

Method: 设计了LLMON标记语言，明确区分文本中的不同成分（如指令、数据、元数据），使LLM能够以自然方式理解文本结构，并将其用于训练、提示和推理阶段。

Result: 提供了初步的实验证据，表明LLMON在模型训练和推理实现两个应用场景中均具有价值，并讨论了该方法在更广泛领域的潜在研究方向。

Conclusion: 类比编程语言中的类型系统，LLMON可服务于多种目的（静态检查、代码生成、动态检查、IDE高亮等），为LLM-native方法开辟了新的研究机会，有望提升模型的准确性、安全性和隐私性。

Abstract: Textual Large Language Models (LLMs) provide a simple and familiar interface: a string of text is used for both input and output. However, the information conveyed to an LLM often has a richer structure and semantics, which is not conveyed in a string. For example, most prompts contain both instructions ("Summarize this paper into a paragraph") and data (the paper to summarize), but these are usually not distinguished when passed to the model. This can lead to model confusion and security risks, such as prompt injection attacks.
  This work addresses this shortcoming by introducing an LLM-native mark-up language, LLMON (LLM Object Notation, pronounced "Lemon"), that enables the structure and semantic metadata of the text to be communicated in a natural way to an LLM. This information can then be used during model training, model prompting, and inference implementation, leading to improvements in model accuracy, safety, and security. This is analogous to how programming language types can be used for many purposes, such as static checking, code generation, dynamic checking, and IDE highlighting.
  We discuss the general design requirements of an LLM-native markup language, introduce the LLMON markup language and show how it meets these design requirements, describe how the information contained in a LLMON artifact can benefit model training and inference implementation, and provide some preliminary empirical evidence of its value for both of these use cases. We also discuss broader issues and research opportunities that are enabled with an LLM-native approach.

</details>


### [129] [On the Economic Implications of Diversity in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22523)
*Sofia Tapias Montana,Ronnie de Souza Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过访谈10名软件专业人员，探索了软件工程团队中多样性（diversity）的经济影响感知。研究发现从业者从成本、收入、市场速度、流程效率、创新和市场契合六个维度理解多样性的经济价值，且多基于具体项目经验而非抽象经济理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程研究领域存在明显缺口：现有研究主要关注多样性的社会技术层面和过程相关结果，缺乏对经济影响的深入探讨。

Method: 采用定性访谈方法，访谈对象为10名软件专业人员，旨在捕捉从业者对多样性经济价值的实际理解和推理过程。

Result: 分析揭示多样性通过六个维度被认为具有经济相关性：成本降低与控制、收入生成、上市时间、流程效率、创新能力、市场契合度。参与者通常基于具体项目经验而非抽象经济推理来支撑这些认知，将多样性视为支持项目交付、竞争力和组织可持续发展的实践资源。

Conclusion: 本研究为软件工程实践中多样性经济层面的理解提供了初步实证依据，表明经济视角在团队多样性研究中值得更多关注。

Abstract: This paper investigates how software professionals perceive the economic implications of diversity in software engineering teams. Motivated by a gap in software engineering research, which has largely emphasized socio-technical and process-related outcomes, we adopted a qualitative interview approach to capture practitioners' reasoning about diversity in relation to economic and market-oriented considerations. Based on interviews with ten software professionals, our analysis indicates that diversity is perceived as economically relevant through its associations with cost reduction and containment, revenue generation, time to market, process efficiency, innovation, and market alignment. Participants typically grounded these perceptions in concrete project experiences rather than abstract economic reasoning, framing diversity as a practical resource that supports project delivery, competitiveness, and organizational viability. Our findings provide preliminary empirical insights into how economic aspects of diversity are understood in software engineering practice.

</details>


### [130] [A Study of Scientific Computational Notebook Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22726)
*Shun Kashiwa,Ayla Kurdak,Savitha Ravi,Ridhi Srikanth,Angel Thakur,Sonia Chandra,Jonathan Truong,Michael Coblenz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析Nature 2024年发表的1239篇论文中的代码可用性声明，筛选出518个代码仓库，并从可重现性、可读性和可重用性三个维度评估科学代码质量。研究发现科学代码质量存在严重问题：在19个尝试执行的Jupyter笔记本中仅有2个可完全重现，主要障碍为缺失数据文件和依赖问题；代码克隆现象普遍，发现326个至少10行的克隆类；变异分析显示科学笔记本常呈现纠缠的状态变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学代码质量是研究社区的关键关切。低质量代码可能导致结果不可重现、错误发现和科学进展减速。然而，目前缺乏对科学代码质量的系统性实证评估。

Method: 研究团队：1) 解析2024年1239篇Nature出版物的代码可用性声明，筛选出518个代码仓库；2) 采用多种评估方法：手动执行Jupyter笔记本、文档审查、代码克隆分析和变异模式分析。

Result: 核心发现：1) 可重现性极低——19个笔记本中仅2个可重现，主要原因是数据文件缺失和依赖问题；2) 代码重复普遍——发现326个至少10行的克隆类，在637个/1510个笔记本中发现3个实例；3) 状态管理混乱——科学笔记本常呈现纠缠的状态变化，增加理解和推理难度。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了科学代码质量的严峻现状：不可重现的代码、广泛的重复和混乱的状态管理。这表明亟需改进的工具和抽象来帮助科学界构建可重现、可读和可重用的软件。

Abstract: The quality of scientific code is a critical concern for the research community. Poorly written code can result in irreproducible results, incorrect findings, and slower scientific progress. In this study, we evaluate scientific code quality across three dimensions: reproducibility, readability, and reusability. We curated a corpus of 518 code repositories by analyzing Code Availability statements from all 1239 Nature publications in 2024. To assess code quality, we employed multiple methods, including manual attempts to reproduce Jupyter notebooks, documentation reviews, and analyses of code clones and mutation patterns. Our results reveal major challenges in scientific code quality. Of the 19 notebooks we attempted to execute, only two were reproducible, primarily due to missing data files and dependency issues. Code duplication was also common, with 326 clone classes of at least 10 lines and three instances found among 637 of the 1510 notebooks in our corpus. These duplications frequently involved tasks such as visualization, data processing, and statistical analysis. Moreover, our mutation analysis showed that scientific notebooks often exhibit tangled state changes, complicating comprehension and reasoning. The prevalence of these issues -- unreproducible code, widespread duplication, and tangled state management -- underscores the need for improved tools and abstractions to help science build reproducible, readable and reusable software.

</details>


### [131] [Machine Learning Models for the Early Detection of Burnout in Software Engineering: a Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23063)
*Tien Rahayu Tulili,Ayushi Rastogi,Andrea Capiluppi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于使用机器学习技术检测软件工程师和IT专业人员职业倦怠的系统文献综述（SLR）。研究主要关注情绪检测与情绪维度分析，并比较了不同ML方法在情绪检测任务上的表现，以识别最具潜力的工具和数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 职业倦怠影响大多数软件工程师，现有研究趋势表明机器学习技术可用于早期检测倦怠，但缺乏对现有ML方法准确性和精度的系统评估，需要为后续研究者提供可复现的推荐方案。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，收集并分析提出ML方法检测软件开发者倦怠的原始研究；同时进行横断面研究，比较不同ML方法在情绪检测任务上的性能表现，以及各数据集的表达潜力。

Result: 多数原始研究聚焦于情绪检测或利用情绪维度来识别倦怠；横断面研究揭示了不同ML方法的性能差异及最具表现力的数据集。

Conclusion: 通过识别表现最佳的ML工具和数据集，可为倦怠检测（尤其是情绪间接识别）领域的后续研究提供有价值的参考，推动该重要研究方向的发展。

Abstract: Burnout is an occupational syndrome that, like many other professions, affects the majority of software engineers. Past research studies showed important trends, including an increasing use of machine learning techniques to allow for an early detection of burnout.
  This paper is a systematic literature review (SLR) of the research papers that proposed machine learning (ML) approaches, and focused on detecting burnout in software developers and IT professionals. Our objective is to review the accuracy and precision of the proposed ML techniques, and to formulate recommendations for future researchers interested to replicate or extend those studies.
  From our SLR we observed that a majority of primary studies focuses on detecting emotions or utilise emotional dimensions to detect or predict the presence of burnout. We also performed a cross-sectional study to detect which ML approach shows a better performance at detecting emotions; and which dataset has more potential and expressivity to capture emotions.
  We believe that, by identifying which ML tools and datasets show a better performance at detecting emotions, and indirectly at identifying burnout, our paper can be a valuable asset to progress in this important research direction.

</details>


### [132] [Can an LLM Detect Instances of Microservice Infrastructure Patterns?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23073)
*Carlos Eduardo Duarte,Neil B. Harrison,Filipe Figueiredo Correia,Ademar Aguiar,Pavlína Gonçalves*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出MicroPAD工具，利用GPT-5 nano基于自然语言描述检测多语言软件制品中的架构模式，在包含190个仓库的人类标注数据集上进行评估。实验表明该工具可跨语言检测模式，但性能因模式而异（F1得分0.09至0.70），与模式流行度和制品特征显著相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前架构模式检测工具存在明显局限：工具种类繁多，且通常仅支持检测单一编程语言编写的制品中的模式。这种局限性促使研究者探索能否利用在大规模软件制品和知识上训练的LLM来克服现有方法的不足。

Method: 研究团队开发了MicroPAD工具，使用GPT-5 nano根据自然语言模式描述来识别任意语言编写的软件制品中的架构模式。具体方法包括：选取GitHub仓库并联系其核心贡献者，创建了包含190个仓库的新型人类标注数据集，重点关注基础设施相关的微服务架构模式。评估涵盖了多种语言和制品类型的模式实例检测。

Result: MicroPAD能够跨多种语言和制品类型检测模式实例。检测性能因模式不同而存在差异（F1得分从0.09到0.70不等），具体与模式的流行度以及模式所通过制品的独特性相关。研究发现，与可识别的主导性制品相关联的模式能够被更可靠地检测出来。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了LLM在多语言架构模式检测方面的可行性，但发现检测效果受到模式特征和制品类型的显著影响。未来研究方向包括：验证这些发现能否推广至其他LLM和工具，以及进一步提升模式检测的准确性和通用性。

Abstract: Architectural patterns are frequently found in various software artifacts. The wide variety of patterns and their implementations makes detection challenging with current tools, especially since they often only support detecting patterns in artifacts written in a single language. Large Language Models (LLMs), trained on a diverse range of software artifacts and knowledge, might overcome the limitations of existing approaches. However, their true effectiveness and the factors influencing their performance have not yet been thoroughly examined. To better understand this, we developed MicroPAD. This tool utilizes GPT 5 nano to identify architectural patterns in software artifacts written in any language, based on natural-language pattern descriptions. We used MicroPAD to evaluate an LLM's ability to detect instances of architectural patterns, particularly infrastructure-related microservice patterns. To accomplish this, we selected a set of GitHub repositories and contacted their top contributors to create a new, human-annotated dataset of 190 repositories containing microservice architectural patterns. The results show that MicroPAD was capable of detecting pattern instances across multiple languages and artifact types. The detection performance varied across patterns (F1 scores ranging from 0.09 to 0.70), specifically in relation to their prevalence and the distinctiveness of the artifacts through which they manifest. We also found that patterns associated with recognizable, dominant artifacts were detected more reliably. Whether these findings generalize to other LLMs and tools is a promising direction for future research.

</details>


### [133] [Fault-Tolerant Design and Multi-Objective Model Checking for Real-Time Deep Reinforcement Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23113)
*Guoxin Su,Thomas Robinson,Hoa Khanh Dam,Li Liu,David S. Rosenblum*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于在实时环境中设计和管理DRL系统的形式化框架，通过将时序自动机转换为MDP进行多目标模型检验，实现安全约束下的性能优化，并开发了GPU加速工具MOPMC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DRL系统在实时环境中面临可靠性挑战，包括仿真-现实差距、分布外观测和延迟关键影响。现有容错方法缺乏形式化方法来严格分析和同时优化实时环境中的性能与安全性。

Method: 提出一种形式化框架，利用时序自动机（TA）设计显式切换逻辑，然后句法转换为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）进行形式化分析。开发了新型凸查询技术用于多目标模型检验，可在确保硬安全约束的同时优化软性能目标。

Result: 开发了MOPMC工具，采用GPU加速实现，在模型规模和目标数量两方面均表现出卓越的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该框架能够为实时DRL系统提供形式化验证能力，同时兼顾安全性和性能优化，为构建高可靠性智能系统提供了新途径。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for solving complex decision-making problems. However, DRL-based systems still face significant dependability challenges particularly in real-time environments due to the simulation-to-reality gap, out-of-distribution observations, and the critical impact of latency. Latency-induced faults, in particular, can lead to unsafe or unstable behaviour, yet existing fault-tolerance approaches to DRL systems lack formal methods to rigorously analyse and optimise performance and safety simultaneously in real-time settings. To address this, we propose a formal framework for designing and analysing real-time switching mechanisms between DRL agents and alternative controllers. Our approach leverages Timed Automata (TAs) for explicit switch logic design, which is then syntactically converted to a Markov Decision Process (MDP) for formal analysis. We develop a novel convex query technique for multi-objective model checking, enabling the optimisation of soft performance objectives while ensuring hard safety constraints for MDPs. Furthermore, we present MOPMC, a GPU-accelerated software tool implementing this technique, demonstrating superior scalability in both model size and objective numbers.

</details>


### [134] [Q-GARS: Quantum-inspired Robust Microservice Chaining Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23127)
*Huixiang Zhang,Mahzabeen Emu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Q-GARS是一种混合微服务调度框架，将QUBO问题公式化与模拟量子退火相结合，通过自适应闭环信任权重机制在SQA先验与鲁棒比例公平分配器之间动态切换，实现全局微服务排序生成与实时鲁棒调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务应用具有随机延迟特性，源于长尾执行模式和跨计算节点异构资源约束，现有调度方法难以有效应对预测失败和运行时扰动。

Method: 1) 将调度问题表述为QUBO形式，与量子优化范式对齐；2) 基于模拟量子退火(SQA)进行组合搜索生成微服务排序；3) 设计自适应信任权重闭环机制，在SQA先验与鲁棒比例公平分配器之间动态切换与混合。

Result: 相比贪婪SRPT基线，Q-GARS加权完成时间平均提升2.1%，重尾延迟场景下提升达16.8%；高方差条件下尾部延迟降低；节点资源平均利用率达0.817，较鲁棒基线提升1.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 混合量子启发式方法与自适应机制可有效平衡全局优化与运行时鲁棒性，为微服务调度提供了一种可行的量子计算过渡期解决方案。

Abstract: Microservice-based applications are characterized by stochastic latencies arising from long-tail execution patterns and heterogeneous resource constraints across computational nodes. To address this challenge, we first formulate the problem using Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO), which aligns the problem with emerging quantum-optimization paradigms. Building upon this, we propose Q-GARS (Quantum-Guided Adaptive Robust Scheduling), a hybrid framework that integrates the QUBO model with Simulated Quantum Annealing (SQA) based combinatorial search and online rescheduling mechanisms, enabling global microservice rank generation and real-time robust adjustment. We treat the SQA-produced rank as a soft prior, and update a closed-loop trust weight to adaptively switch and mix between this prior and a robust proportional-fairness allocator, maintaining robustness under prediction failures and runtime disturbances. Simulation results demonstrate that Q-GARS achieves an average weighted completion time improvement of 2.1\% relative to a greedy baseline of the remaining shortest processing-time (SRPT), with performance gains reaching up to 16.8\% in heavy-tailed latency. The adaptive mechanism reduces tail latency under high-variance conditions. In addition, Q-GARS achieves a mean node resource utilization rate of 0.817, which is 1.1 percentage points above the robust baseline (0.806).

</details>


### [135] [Can Language Models Pass Software Testing Certification Exams? a case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23142)
*Fitash Ul Haq,Jordi Cabot*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: This study evaluates 60 multimodal LLMs on their ability to pass software testing certification exams (ISTQB), testing both foundational knowledge and robustness under metamorphic transformations. Commercial models outperformed open-source ones, with only two models achieving passing scores (≥65%) across all 30 certification exams.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Despite the growing use of LLMs in software testing activities (test generation, selection, repair), it remains unclear whether LLMs truly understand software testing principles or can reliably answer software testing questions, especially under conceptual transformations. This study addresses this critical gap by systematically evaluating LLM capabilities on standardized software testing certifications.

Method: 60 multimodal LLMs (commercial and open-source) were evaluated on 1,171 questions from 30 ISTQB certification exams spanning four difficulty levels (core foundation, core advanced, specialist, expert). Additionally, exam questions were transformed using context-preserving metamorphic techniques to assess conceptual understanding robustness. Each model answered the full question set under standardized conditions.

Result: Two models passed all certifications by scoring ≥65% on all 30 exams. Commercial models generally outperformed open-source models. Analysis of incorrect answers revealed specific conceptual weaknesses and patterns influenced by question properties.

Conclusion: LLMs show promise in software testing certification but struggle with certain conceptual transformations and question types. The findings provide actionable recommendations for improving both LLM design and the design of software testing certification exams. The benchmark dataset and metamorphic transformations offer a robust evaluation framework for future LLM assessment in software testing domains.

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) play a pivotal role in both academic research and broader societal applications. LLMs are increasingly used in software testing activities such as test case generation, selection, and repair. However, several important questions remain: (1) do LLMs possess enough information about software testing principles to perform software testing tasks effectively? (2) do LLMs possess sufficient conceptual understanding of software testing to answer software testing questions under metamorphic transformations? and (3) do certain properties of software testing questions influence the performance of LLMs? To answer these questions, this study evaluates 60 multimodal language models from both commercial vendors and the open-source community. The evaluation is performed using 30 sample exams of different types (core foundation, core advanced, specialist, and expert) from the International Software Testing Qualifications Board (ISTQB), which are used to assess the competence of human testers. In total, each model is evaluated on 1,171 questions. Furthermore, to ensure sufficient conceptual understanding, the models are also tested on exam questions transformed using context-preserving metamorphic techniques. Two models passed all the certifications by scoring at least 65% in all of the 30 certification exams, with commercial models generally outperforming open-source ones. We analyze the reasons behind incorrect answers and provide recommendations for improving the design of software testing certification exams.

</details>


### [136] [Rethinking Self-Sovereign Identity Principles: An Actor-Oriented Categorization of Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23177)
*Daria Schumm,Burkhard Stiller*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对去中心化身份(DI)和自主权身份(SSI)系统，将 SSI 原则分解为 24 个非功能需求(NFR)，并系统性地映射到数据所有者、发行者、验证者和系统四类关键角色，引入了依赖模型来形式化角色间的关系，为 DI/SSI 系统架构提供了首个结构化模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前集中式身份管理系统面临安全与隐私挑战，DI/SSI 作为用户导向的替代方案应运而生。现有研究虽已整合 SSI 原则并推导出质量需求，但在整合用户视角方面存在显著不足。

Method: 1. 基于现有 SSI 原则；2. 将复合需求分解为 24 个简单质量/NFR；3. 基于责任与所有权程度，系统性映射 NFR 到四类关键角色（数据所有者、发行者、验证者、系统）；4. 引入依赖模型形式化角色间交互关系，借鉴信任建模概念描述角色间如何相互作用以满足需求。

Result: 将用户中心需求、责任分配、所有权规范和依赖建模整合，形成 DI/SSI 系统架构的首个结构化模型。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了 DI/SSI 需求工程中用户视角缺失的空白，通过依赖模型明确描述了角色交互与需求满足机制，为后续系统设计与实现提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Centralized identity management systems continuously experience security and privacy challenges, motivating the exploration of Decentralized Identity (DI) and Self-Sovereign Identity (SSI) as user-focused alternatives. Although prior research has consolidated SSI principles and derived quality requirements for DI/SSI systems, it is significantly limited in integrating the user viewpoint. This work addresses this gap by embedding a user perspective into the requirements engineering process for DI/SSI systems. Building on existing SSI principles, composite requirements were decomposed into 24 simple quality or non-functional requirements (NFR). The resulting NFR are systematically mapped to the key actors, namely data owner, issuer, verifier, and system, based on varying degrees of responsibility and ownership. A dependency model is introduced to formalize relationships between actors. Inspired by trust modeling concepts, the model explicitly describes how actors interact and rely on each other for requirements fulfillment. By integrating user-centered requirements, responsibility allocation, ownership specification, and dependency modeling, this work provides the first structured model for DI/SSI system architectures.

</details>


### [137] [MuSe: a Mutation Testing Plugin for the Remix IDE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23441)
*Gerardo Iuliano,Daniele Carangelo,Carmine Calabrese,Dario Di Nucci*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MuSe是一个面向Remix-IDE的智能合约变异测试插件，集成了传统、Solidity特定和安全导向三类变异算子，旨在帮助开发者评估测试套件有效性、识别智能合约潜在安全缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变异测试是评估测试套件有效性的重要手段，但Remix-IDE（最广泛使用的以太坊智能合约开发环境）尚无此类工具。智能合约安全至关重要，小缺陷即可导致重大经济损失。

Method: 提出MuSe插件，集成三类变异算子：传统变异算子、Solidity特定变异算子、安全导向变异算子。基于Remix-IDE集成开发，无需额外配置，降低使用门槛。

Result: 开发者可立即利用MuSe进行变异测试，评估测试套件有效性并识别智能合约中的潜在问题。

Conclusion: MuSe填补了Remix-IDE变异测试工具空白，为智能合约质量保障提供了便捷手段。提供了演示视频和开源仓库。

Abstract: Mutation testing is a technique to assess the effectiveness of test suites by introducing artificial faults into programs. Although mutation testing plugins are available for many platforms and languages, none is currently available for Remix-IDE, the most widely used Integrated Development Environment for the entire contract development journey, used by users of all knowledge levels, and serves as a learning lab for teaching and experimenting with Ethereum. The quality and security of smart contracts are crucial in blockchain systems, as even minor issues can result in substantial financial losses. This paper proposes MuSe, a mutation testing plugin for the Remix-IDE. MuSe includes traditional, Solidity-specific, and security-oriented mutation operators. Its integration into the Remix-IDE eliminates the need for additional setup and lowers the entry barrier. As a result, developers and researchers can immediately leverage mutation testing to assess the effectiveness of their test suites and identify potential issues in smart contracts. We provide a demo video showing MuSe: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=MIFk9exTDu0 and its repository: https://github.com/GerardoIuliano/MuSe-Remix-Plugin.

</details>


### [138] [Evaluating LLM-Based Test Generation Under Software Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23443)
*Sabaat Haroon,Mohammad Taha Khan,Muhammad Ali Gulzar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究，分析了大语言模型(LLM)在程序变化下的单元测试生成能力。研究发现LLM在原始程序上表现良好(79%行覆盖率、76%分支覆盖率)，但在程序演进时性能显著下降：语义变更下通过率降至66%、分支覆盖率降至60%；语义保持变更下通过率降至79%、分支覆盖率降至69%。研究表明当前LLM测试生成主要依赖表层线索而非深层语义理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在自动化单元测试生成中的广泛应用，其生成的测试是否真正反映程序行为的推理，还是仅复制训练中习得的表面模式，这一问题尚不明确。理解LLM如何生成测试以及测试如何响应代码演进至关重要。

Method: 使用自动化突变驱动框架，对8个LLM和22,374个程序变体进行大规模实证研究，分析生成的测试对语义变更(SAC)和语义保持变更(SPC)的反应。

Result: LLM在原始程序上达到79%行覆盖率和76%分支覆盖率的良好基线。SAC下新生成测试通过率降至66%，分支覆盖率降至60%，超过99%的失败测试在原程序上通过但执行修改区域，表明测试与原行为对齐而非适应新语义。SPC下尽管功能不变，但通过率降至79%、分支覆盖率降至69%，因语义保持变更常引入较大语法变化，导致测试套件不稳定。

Conclusion: 当前基于LLM的测试生成严重依赖表层线索，对词汇变化敏感而非真正的语义影响，在程序演进时难以保持回归感知能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for automated unit test generation. However, it remains unclear whether these tests reflect genuine reasoning about program behavior or simply reproduce superficial patterns learned during training. If the latter dominates, LLM-generated tests may exhibit weaknesses such as reduced coverage, missed regressions, and undetected faults. Understanding how LLMs generate tests and how those tests respond to code evolution is therefore essential. We present a large-scale empirical study of LLM-based test generation under program changes. Using an automated mutation-driven framework, we analyze how generated tests react to semantic-altering changes (SAC) and semantic-preserving changes (SPC) across eight LLMs and 22,374 program variants.
  LLMs achieve strong baseline results, reaching 79% line coverage and 76% branch coverage with fully passing test suites on the original programs. However, performance degrades as programs evolve. Under SACs, the pass rate of newly generated tests drops to 66%, and branch coverage declines to 60%. More than 99% of failing SAC tests pass on the original program while executing the modified region, indicating residual alignment with the original behavior rather than adaptation to updated semantics. Performance also declines under SPCs despite unchanged functionality: pass rates fall to 79% and branch coverage to 69%. Although SPC edits preserve semantics, they often introduce larger syntactic changes, leading to instability in generated test suites. Models generate more new tests while discarding many baseline tests, suggesting sensitivity to lexical changes rather than true semantic impact. Overall, our results indicate that current LLM-based test generation relies heavily on surface-level cues and struggles to maintain regression awareness as programs evolve.

</details>


### [139] [Code Review Agent Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23448)
*Yuntong Zhang,Zhiyuan Pan,Imam Nur Bani Yusuf,Haifeng Ruan,Ridwan Shariffdeen,Abhik Roychoudhury*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Software engineering agents have shown significant promise in writing code. As AI agents permeate code writing, and generate huge volumes of code automatically -- the matter of code quality comes front and centre. As the automatically generated code gets integrated into huge code-bases -- the issue of code review and broadly quality assurance becomes important. In this paper, we take a fresh look at the problem and curate a code review dataset for AI agents to work with. Our dataset called c-CRAB (pronounced see-crab) can evaluate agents for code review tasks. Specifically given a pull-request (which could be coming from code generation agents or humans), if a code review agent produces a review, our evaluation framework can asses the reviewing capability of the code review agents. Our evaluation framework is used to evaluate the state of the art today -- the open-source PR-agent, as well as commercial code review agents from Devin, Claude Code, and Codex.
  Our c-CRAB dataset is systematically constructed from human reviews -- given a human review of a pull request instance we generate corresponding tests to evaluate the code review agent generated reviews. Such a benchmark construction gives us several insights. Firstly, the existing review agents taken together can solve only around 40% of the c-CRAB tasks, indicating the potential to close this gap by future research. Secondly, we observe that the agent reviews often consider different aspects from the human reviews -- indicating the potential for human-agent collaboration for code review that could be deployed in future software teams. Last but not the least, the agent generated tests from our data-set act as a held out test-suite and hence quality gate for agent generated reviews. What this will mean for future collaboration of code generation agents, test generation agents and code review agents -- remains to be investigated.

</details>


### [140] [ConceptCoder: Improve Code Reasoning via Concept Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23470)
*Md Mahbubur Rahman,Hengbo Tong,Wei Le*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ConceptCoder是一种新颖的微调方法，通过模拟人类代码审查过程——先识别代码概念，再基于概念进行推理——来提升代码漏洞检测（VD）性能。该方法将代码概念定义为人类可理解的代码语义属性，在9个开源LLM上平均将F1从66.32提升至72.15，并超越GPT-5.2和Claude-Opus-4.5等专有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型在软件工程应用中表现出色，但在代码推理任务（如漏洞检测）上仍存在不足。现有方法缺乏对代码深层语义的理解能力，无法像人类工程师那样先识别代码概念再进行推理分析。

Method: 提出ConceptCoder微调方法：1）将代码概念定义为人类可理解的代码语义属性；2）训练模型识别代码概念；3）在概念基础上执行推理任务。实验涵盖9个开源LLM，并在134个CWE漏洞类型的数据集上验证泛化能力。

Result: 漏洞检测F1从66.32提升至72.15（平均）；超越SOTA基线包括微调开源LLM和GPT-5.2、Claude-Opus-4.5等提示式专有模型；4种漏洞类型的概念定义可泛化至134个CWE；概念微调可迁移至分支预测任务。

Conclusion: ConceptCoder通过概念驱动的微调范式有效提升代码推理能力，证明代码概念作为语义桥梁的可行性。该方法具有良好的可扩展性和任务迁移性，为代码智能分析提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promising results for software engineering applications, but still struggle with code reasoning tasks such as vulnerability detection (VD). We introduce ConceptCoder, a fine-tuning method that simulates human code inspection: models are trained to first recognize code concepts and then perform reasoning on top of these concepts. In prior work, concepts are extracted by multimodal models or LLMs to explain vision and natural language models. Our work is the first to formulate concepts for code. We define code concepts as human-understandable semantic properties of code and train models to learn such concepts. Our evaluation shows that this approach significantly improves VD accuracy, from 66.32 to 72.15 F1 on average over 9 open-source LLMs. ConceptCoder achieves the best VD performance compared to state-of-the-art (SOTA) baselines, including fine-tuned SOTA open-source LLMs and prompted proprietary models such as GPT-5.2 and Claude-Opus-4.5. Our approach also scales: concepts defined from four types of vulnerabilities benefit general vulnerability datasets with 134 CWEs. We further demonstrate that concept-based fine-tuning generalizes beyond VD and improves branch prediction. We release our code and datasets at https://figshare.com/s/1decab8232c653b44f71.

</details>


### [141] [ReqFusion: A Multi-Provider Framework for Automated PEGS Analysis Across Software Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23482)
*Muhammad Khalid,Manuel Oriol,Yilmaz Uygun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ReqFusion是一个利用多个LLM提供商（GPT、Claude、Groq）自动提取和分析软件需求的AI系统，采用PEGS框架引导生成，在226条需求测试中F1达0.88，较通用提示提升24%，并实现78%的时间节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求工程是软件开发的关重阶段，但人工处理效率低下，需要自动化工具提升效率。

Method: 集成多LLM提供商架构，使用PEGS格式引导提示词，从多格式文档中提取功能和非功能需求，采用模型共识和回退机制确保可靠性。

Result: PEGS引导F1=0.88 vs 通用提示F1=0.71；226条需求（54.9%功能/45.1%非功能）；扩展评估1,050条需求；分析时间减少78%。

Conclusion: PEGS引导策略显著优于通用提示；多提供商架构增强系统可靠性；系统实现显著效率提升和全面的需求覆盖。

Abstract: Requirements engineering is a vital, yet labor-intensive, stage in the software development process. This article introduces ReqFusion: an AI-enhanced system that automates the extraction, classification, and analysis of software requirements utilizing multiple Large Language Model (LLM) providers. The architecture of ReqFusion integrates OpenAI GPT, Anthropic Claude, and Groq models to extract functional and non-functional requirements from various documentation formats (PDF, DOCX, and PPTX) in academic, industrial, and tender proposal contexts. The system uses a domain-independent extraction method and generates requirements following the Project, Environment, Goal, and System (PEGS) approach introduced by Bertrand Meyer. The main idea is that, because the PEGS format is detailed, LLMs have more information and cues about the requirements, producing better results than a simple generic request. An ablation study confirms this hypothesis: PEGS-guided prompting achieves an F1 score of 0.88, compared to 0.71 for generic prompting under the same multi-provider configuration. The evaluation used 18 real-world documents to generate 226 requirements through automated classification, with 54.9% functional and 45.1% nonfunctional across academic, business, and technical domains. An extended evaluation on five projects with 1,050 requirements demonstrated significant improvements in extraction accuracy and a 78% reduction in analysis time compared to manual methods. The multi-provider architecture enhances reliability through model consensus and fallback mechanisms, while the PEGS-based approach ensures comprehensive coverage of all requirement categories.

</details>


### [142] [Automated Microservice Pattern Instance Detection Using Infrastructure-as-Code Artifacts and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.04188)
*Carlos Eduardo Duarte*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为MicroPAD的原型工具，利用大型语言模型(LLM)分析基础设施即代码(IaC)制品，自动检测微服务模式实例，旨在降低架构知识文档化的成本和门槛。初步实验在22个GitHub项目中验证了该方法的可行性（83%准确率），有望帮助开发者更便捷地获取架构知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件架构知识的保存至关重要，但文档化成本高昂。架构模式实例包含重要的结构化软件信息，但现有模式检测方法难以通过源代码直接检测，且扩展性差。需要一种低成本、高扩展性的自动化检测方法。

Method: 使用大型语言模型(LLM)分析基础设施即代码(IaC)制品，实现微服务模式实例的自动检测。通过在22个GitHub项目上进行三次实验验证原型工具MicroPAD的有效性。

Result: 原型工具MicroPAD在22个GitHub项目中检测到83%的模式实例被验证存在，检测成本极低，证明了该方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 基于LLM和IaC的微服务模式实例检测方法具有可行性。该方法降低了自动化模式实例检测的准入门槛，有望帮助开发者更便捷地获取架构知识类别，具有工业应用潜力。未来将进行更多研究工作以完善该工具。

Abstract: Documenting software architecture is essential to preserve architecture knowledge, even though it is frequently costly. Architecture pattern instances, including microservice pattern instances, provide important structural software information. Practitioners should document this information to prevent knowledge vaporization. However, architecture patterns may not be detectable by analyzing source code artifacts, requiring the analysis of other types of artifacts. Moreover, many existing pattern detection instance approaches are complex to extend. This article presents our ongoing PhD research, early experiments, and a prototype for a tool we call MicroPAD for automating the detection of microservice pattern instances. The prototype uses Large Language Models (LLMs) to analyze Infrastructure-as-Code (IaC) artifacts to aid detection, aiming to keep costs low and maximize the scope of detectable patterns. Early experiments ran the prototype thrice in 22 GitHub projects. We verified that 83\% of the patterns that the prototype identified were in the project. The costs of detecting the pattern instances were minimal. These results indicate that the approach is likely viable and, by lowering the entry barrier to automating pattern instance detection, could help democratize developer access to this category of architecture knowledge. Finally, we present our overall research methodology, planned future work, and an overview of MicroPAD's potential industrial impact.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [Interactive and Urgent HPC: State of the Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22542)
*Albert Reuther,William Arndt,Johannes Blaschke,Christian Boehme,Nick Brown,Antony Chazapis,Bjoern Enders,Jens Henrik Goebbert,Robert Henschel,Julian Kunkel,Maxime Martinasso,Michael Ringenburg,Rollin Thomas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文讨论了交互式和紧迫计算趋势如何从传统计算领域（智能手机、电子商务、协作平台、大语言模型）扩展到高性能计算（HPC）领域，涵盖模拟、数据分析和机器学习等应用场景，并展望了交互式与紧迫型HPC策略、技术和生态系统集成的未来发展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算领域已普遍采用交互式和紧迫型计算模式，而HPC领域正面临类似的转型需求。模拟、数据分析和机器学习等应用对并行计算能力和交互性的要求日益增长，需要将交互式HPC政策、技术和工具整合到现有HPC生态系统中。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，回顾了交互式和紧迫型HPC领域的已有进展，并展望了未来发展方向，旨在为HPC生态系统集成提供指导。

Result: 文章提供了交互式和紧迫型HPC领域的发展概览，识别了当前进展与未来发展路径。

Conclusion: 需要进一步推动交互式和紧迫型HPC政策、技术和工具与现有HPC生态系统的深度融合，以满足现代应用对实时交互计算的需求。

Abstract: When we think of how we use smartphones, e-commerce, collaboration platforms, LLMs, etc., most of our interactions with computers are interactive and often urgent. Similar trends of interactivity and urgency are coming to HPC, with applications from simulations to data analysis and machine learning requiring more parallel computational capability and more interactivity. This chapter overviews the progress made so far along with some vectors of what the path forward will bring for greater integration of interactive and urgent HPC policies, techniques, and technologies into our HPC ecosystems.

</details>


### [144] [Rank-Aware Resource Scheduling for Tightly-Coupled MPI Workloads on Kubernetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22691)
*Tianfang Xie*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对云托管Kubernetes集群上的MPI CFD求解器，提出CPU请求与子域网格数量成比例的rank感知资源调度，结合原地Pod垂直扩展实现模拟中CPU调整。关键发现：Linux CFS硬CPU限制导致MPI_Allreduce同步点出现78倍性能下降，而仅使用CPU请求（不设硬限制）可完全消除节流；等比例CPU分配比Scotch基线快19%，进一步采用比例分配再提升3%；在16核101K网格场景下，比例分配比等比例分配快20%，低负载子域CPU配置减少82%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云共享集群中，低负载子域预留过多CPU资源导致集群打包效率低下，需探索细粒度CPU配置在保持性能的同时提升资源利用率的可能性

Method: 在Kubernetes上实现rank感知资源调度，将每个MPI rank映射到CPU请求与子域网格数量成比例的Pod；利用Kubernetes v1.35的原地Pod垂直扩展实现运行时CPU调整；通过AWS EC2 c5.xlarge集群（4-16 ranks）对比测试CFS带宽控制器的硬限制与仅请求分配方案，以及等比例与比例CPU分配策略

Result: （1）Linux CFS硬CPU限制在MPI_Allreduce屏障处引发级联停顿，导致78倍性能下降；（2）在非突发型c5.xlarge实例上，同心分解配合等CPU分配比Scotch基线快19%，比例CPU分配再提升3%；（3）16核101K网格场景下，比例分配比等比例分配快20%，稀疏子域CPU配置减少82%，释放6.5 vCPU调度余量

Conclusion: 在Kubernetes上运行MPI CFD求解器时，仅使用CPU请求（不设硬限制）可完全消除节流风险；CPU请求与子域计算负载成比例的分配策略能够同时提升性能并显著降低资源预留，实现性能与集群效率的双赢

Abstract: Fully provisioned Message Passing Interface (MPI) parallelism achieves near-optimal wall-clock time for Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) solvers. This work addresses a complementary question for shared, cloud-managed clusters: can fine-grained CPU provisioning reduce resource reservation of low-load subdomains, improving cluster packing efficiency without unacceptably degrading performance?
  We propose rank-aware resource scheduling on Kubernetes, mapping each MPI rank to a pod whose CPU request is proportional to its subdomain cell count. We also demonstrate In-Place Pod Vertical Scaling (Kubernetes v1.35 GA) for mid-simulation CPU adjustment without pod restart.
  Three findings emerge. First, hard CPU limits via the Linux CFS bandwidth controller cause 78x slowdown through cascading stalls at MPI_Allreduce barriers; requests-only allocation eliminates throttling entirely. Second, on non-burstable c5.xlarge instances, concentric decomposition with equal CPU is 19% faster than the Scotch baseline, while adding proportional CPU yields a further 3% improvement. Third, at 16 MPI ranks on 101K-cell meshes, proportional allocation is 20% faster than equal allocation while reducing sparse-subdomain provisioned CPU by 82%, freeing 6.5 vCPU of scheduling headroom.
  Experiments are conducted on AWS EC2 c5.xlarge clusters (4-16 ranks) running k3s v1.35. All scripts and data are released as open source.

</details>


### [145] [PCR: A Prefetch-Enhanced Cache Reuse System for Low-Latency RAG Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23049)
*Wenfeng Wang,Xiaofeng Hou,Peng Tang,Hengyi Zhou,Jing Wang,Xinkai Wang,Chao Li,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PCR是一种优化RAG系统中KV-cache复用效率的系统，通过智能预取和流水线数据传输技术，在TTFT指标上实现最高2.47倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统因整合外部文档导致输入序列过长，在prefill阶段产生高计算成本和高延迟。KV-cache复用虽可避免冗余计算，但面临三大实际挑战：缓存命中率低（淘汰策略简单）、CPU-GPU数据传输开销高、以及缓存溢出到存储时的SSD I/O慢。

Method: 提出三种关键技术：1）基于前缀树的缓存结构结合前瞻LRU替换策略，利用调度器队列中的待处理请求提升缓存命中率；2）层级重叠，通过CUDA流在KV-cache加载和GPU计算间建立流水线以隐藏通信延迟；3）基于队列的预取机制，主动将KV-cache从SSD预加载到DRAM。

Result: 实验表明，PCR在平均TTFT指标上相比现有KV-cache复用方法实现最高2.47倍加速。

Conclusion: PCR通过智能预取和流水线数据移动技术，有效解决了KV-cache复用面临的三大实际挑战，显著提升了高吞吐量服务场景下的系统性能。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems enhance the performance of large language models (LLMs) by incorporating supplementary retrieved documents, enabling more accurate and context-aware responses. However, integrating these external documents often results in very long input sequences, which significantly increases computation costs during the prefill stage, where key-value (KV) representations for all input tokens are generated. This latency bottleneck becomes especially pronounced under high-throughput serving scenarios. KV-cache reuse offers a promising solution by storing previously computed KV states for shared input prefixes, thereby avoiding redundant computation across requests that contain overlapping context. Yet, the effectiveness of cache reuse is often limited by three practical challenges: low cache hit rates due to naive eviction policies, high CPU-GPU data transfer overhead, and slow SSD I/O when caches spill to storage. To address these issues, we propose PCR, a system designed to maximize KV-cache reuse efficiency through intelligent prefetching and pipelined data movement. Specifically, PCR introduces three key techniques: (1) a prefix-tree caching structure with a look-ahead LRU replacement policy that uses pending requests in the scheduler queue to improve cache hit ratios; (2) layer-wise overlapping that pipelines KV-cache loading and GPU computation across CUDA streams to hide communication latency; and (3) queue-based prefetching that proactively loads relevant KV caches from SSD into DRAM before they are needed. Extensive experiments show that PCR outperforms existing KV-cache reuse methods, achieving up to a 2.47x speedup in terms of average TTFT.

</details>


### [146] [Communication-Aware Diffusion Load Balancing for Persistently Interacting Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23329)
*Maya Taylor,Kavitha Chandrasekar,Laxmikant V. Kale*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对通信密集型应用的分布式扩散式动态负载均衡算法，通过利用应用通信图同时实现减少跨节点通信和有效分配负载，并提供了通信模式不可用时的变体算法。在Perlmutter超级计算机上通过Particle-in-Cell基准测试进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有不规则和时变工作负载的并行应用常遭受负载不均衡问题，动态负载均衡技术虽然在执行期间重新分配工作可以解决此问题，但对于通信密集型应用，传统方法可能无法有效平衡通信开销与计算负载。

Method: 提出基于通信图的分布式扩散式负载均衡算法；利用应用通信图引导负载重新分配以减少跨节点通信；同时提供通信模式不可用时的变体算法；通过模拟和实际基准测试进行评估。

Result: 算法在模拟和实际基准测试中展现良好性能，在NERSC的Perlmutter上使用多达8个节点进行Particle-in-Cell基准测试验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于通信图的扩散式负载均衡策略能有效平衡通信密集型应用的负载，同时最小化通信开销，为高性能计算中的动态负载均衡提供了有价值的解决方案。

Abstract: Parallel applications with irregular and time-varying workloads often suffer from load imbalance. Dynamic load balancing techniques address this challenge by redistributing work during execution. We present a new type of distributed diffusion-based load balancing targeted at communication-intensive applications with persistently communicating objects. Leveraging the application's communication graph, our strategy reduces across-node communication while simultaneously distributing load effectively. We also propose an algorithmic variant for cases where the communication patterns are not readily available. We explore optimizations to our algorithm, and comparisons with other related load balancing strategies in simulation and on a Particle-in-Cell benchmark on up to 8 nodes of Perlmutter at NERSC.

</details>
